dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/main/docs/weathernext1_graph/README.md\"\u003eWeatherNext Graph\u003c/a\u003e: Deterministic\nmedium-range weather forecasting using graph neural networks. Published as\nGraphCast.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/main/docs/weathernext1_gen/README.md\"\u003eWeatherNext Gen\u003c/a\u003e: Diffusion-based ensemble\nforecasting for medium-range weather. The only difference between them is that WN2 can also\npredict 100m wind. In particular, WN2 also forecasts cyclones with the exact\nsame algorithm as WN Cyclones. Their weights are different due to independent\ntraining runs.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWeatherNext 2\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-weathernext-2-1\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: WeatherNext 2\" href=\"#weathernext-2-1\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWeatherNext2_\u0026lt;2025\u003c/strong\u003e (Used Operationally): 0.25° resolution (~30km).\nFine-tuned on ECMWF HRES data and designed to be initialized directly from\noperational HRES initial conditions rather than ERA5 reanalysis. Trained on\ndata through 2024. Corresponding weights files:\n\u003ccode\u003eWeatherNext2_\u0026lt;2025_model{1,2,3,4}.npz\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWeatherNext Cyclones - models which reproduce the results in paper\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-weathernext-cyclones---models-which-reproduce-the-results-in-paper\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: WeatherNext Cyclones - models which reproduce the results in paper\" href=\"#weathernext-cyclones---models-which-reproduce-the-results-in-paper\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_\u0026lt;2025\u003c/strong\u003e (Used Operationally): 0.25° resolution. The\nmodel that ran live during the 2025 Atlantic hurricane season, publicly\nreferred to as FNV3 (NHC's postprocessed version was called GDMI). Trained\non data through 2024. The paper appendix contains a partial evaluation of\n2025 in NHC basins for this model checkpoint, and how the tracker\nimprovement in September 2025 improved results. Corresponding weights files:\n\u003ccode\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_\u0026lt;2025_model{1,2,3,4}.npz\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_\u0026lt;2024\u003c/strong\u003e: 0.25° resolution. Reproduces results from the\npaper on 2024. Trained on data through 2023. Corresponding weights files:\n\u003ccode\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_\u0026lt;2024_model{1,2,3,4}.npz\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_\u0026lt;2023\u003c/strong\u003e: 0.25° resolution. Reproduces results from the\npaper on 2023. Trained on data through 2022. Corresponding weights files:\n\u003ccode\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_\u0026lt;2023_model{1,2,3,4}.npz\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWeatherNext Cyclones Mini\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-weathernext-cyclones-mini\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: WeatherNext Cyclones Mini\" href=\"#weathernext-cyclones-mini\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_Mini_\u0026lt;2024\u003c/strong\u003e: 1° resolution. A lightweight version\nsuitable for lower memory and compute constraints (e.g., local testing or\nsingle TPUs or GPUs). Not expected to match the performance of the larger\nversions. Forecasts the same things as WeatherNext2_\u0026lt;2025, including\ncyclones. Trained on data through 2023. Corresponding weights file:\n\u003ccode\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_Mini_\u0026lt;2024.npz\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_Mini_\u0026lt;2023\u003c/strong\u003e: As above, but only trained on data through\n2022. Corresponding weights file: \u003ccode\u003eWeatherNextCyclones_Mini_\u0026lt;2023.npz\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEvaluation results for WeatherNextCyclones_Mini can be found in the appendix\nof the\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10953-2\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eWeatherNext Cyclones Paper\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuick Start Guide\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-quick-start-guide\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Quick Start Guide\" href=\"#quick-start-guide\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe easiest way to get started with WeatherNext 2 is by running our interactive\n\u003ca href=\"/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/main/docs/weathernext2/wn2_demo.ipynb\"\u003eColab Notebook\u003c/a\u003e, which can be opened from\n\u003ca href=\"https://colab.research.google.com/github/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/master/docs/weathernext2/wn2_demo.ipynb\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eColaboratory\u003c/a\u003e.\nThis notebook defaults to WeatherNext Cyclones Mini, which we recommend running\nusing the \u003ccode\u003ev5e-1\u003c/code\u003e runtime, available for free as a Colab runtime. However, the\nnotebook can also be used to run the other models enumerated above (but these\nwill require a \u003ccode\u003ev5p\u003c/code\u003e accelerator).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn general, we recommend running WeatherNext 2 on TPU where possible, since its\nimplementation has been optimised for it. However, if choosing to run on GPU,\nthe attention implementation must be switched, as shown in the demo notebook.\nThe non-Mini models require H100 for sufficient VRAM. The Mini models should\nmanage inference on a P100.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePre-trained weights and sample data are available on our \u003ca href=\"https://console.cloud.google.com/storage/browser/dm_graphcast\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eGoogle Cloud\nBucket\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInside the notebook, you will learn how to:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAutomatically load the required model weights from our storage bucket.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLoad initial state weather data (e.g., HRES initial conditions).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInitialize the WN2 (FGN) architecture.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun auto-regressive rollout steps to generate a forecast prediction.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVisualize the outputs (e.g., temperature, wind speed, geopotential height).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun the direct tracker on model outputs to obtain track data for cyclones.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCompute the training loss on model predictions and targets, and take a\ngradient step.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSetup\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-setup\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Setup\" href=\"#setup\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 WeatherNext models communicate with the following separate libraries and\npackages:.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eData and products of the European Centre for Medium-range Track Archive for Climate Stewardship (IBTrACS)\ndata, first accessed on 1 Dec 2022.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAdditionally, the colab notebooks include a few examples of ECMWF’s ERA5 and\nHRES data that can be used as input to the models.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNeither the European Commission nor ECMWF is responsible for any use that may be\nmade of the Copernicus information or data it contains. <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">WeatherNext 2</h2><a id="user-content-weathernext-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: WeatherNext 2" href="#weathernext-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This repo contains the code for WeatherNext 2 (WN2), the global, medium-range
atmospheric and cyclone forecasting model developed by Google DeepMind and
Google Research.</p>
<p dir="auto">It also contains code for prior generation models
<a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/graphcast-ai-model-for-faster-and-more-accurate-global-weather-forecasting/" rel="nofollow">GraphCast</a>
and
<a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/gencast-predicts-weather-and-the-risks-of-extreme-conditions-with-sota-accuracy/" rel="nofollow">GenCast</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Accessing Forecast Data Feeds</h3><a id="user-content-accessing-forecast-data-feeds" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Accessing Forecast Data Feeds" href="#accessing-forecast-data-feeds"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you are interested in directly accessing daily data feeds of WN2 model
outputs rather than running the model yourself, we provide them across multiple
platforms:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://developers.google.com/weathernext/guides/access-forecast" rel="nofollow">Google Cloud</a>
(including Earth Engine, BigQuery, and Vertex AI).</li>
<li><a href="https://deepmind.google.com/science/weatherlab" rel="nofollow">WeatherLab</a> (including cyclone tracks).</li>
<li><a href="https://open-meteo.com/en/docs/google-weathernext-api" rel="nofollow">OpenMeteo</a> (including an API and interactive builder).</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Learn More</h3><a id="user-content-learn-more" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Learn More" href="#learn-more"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Model Guide &amp; Documentation:</strong> <a href="https://developers.google.com/weathernext/guides/models" rel="nofollow">Google Developers WeatherNext Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>WeatherNext Cyclones Paper:</strong> <a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10953-2" rel="nofollow">Operational tropical cyclone forecasting
with AI</a></li>
<li><strong>FGN/WN2 Technical Report:</strong> <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10772" rel="nofollow">Skillful joint probabilistic weather
forecasting from marginals
(arXiv:2506.10772)</a></li>
<li><strong>WeatherNext 2 Blog Post:</strong> <a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/google-deepmind/weathernext-2/" rel="nofollow">WeatherNext 2: Our most advanced weather forecasting model</a></li>
<li><strong>WeatherNext Cyclones Blog Post:</strong> <a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/weathernext-ai-model-achieves-breakthrough-in-forecasting-cyclones/" rel="nofollow">WeatherNext: AI model achieves breakthrough in forecasting cyclones</a></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Older Models</h3><a id="user-content-older-models" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Older Models" href="#older-models"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This repository serves as the primary home for the WeatherNext family models.
Alongside WN2, this repository also hosts the code and documentation for our
legacy and specialized models:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/main/docs/weathernext1_graph/README.md">WeatherNext Graph</a>: Deterministic
medium-range weather forecasting using graph neural networks. Published as
GraphCast.</li>
<li><a href="/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/main/docs/weathernext1_gen/README.md">WeatherNext Gen</a>: Diffusion-based ensemble
forecasting for medium-range weather. Published as GenCast.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Provided Pretrained Models</h2><a id="user-content-provided-pretrained-models" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Provided Pretrained Models" href="#provided-pretrained-models"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This repository provides code to run the different versions of WeatherNext 2 and
WeatherNext Cyclones. The only difference between them is that WN2 can also
predict 100m wind. In particular, WN2 also forecasts cyclones with the exact
same algorithm as WN Cyclones. Their weights are different due to independent
training runs.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">WeatherNext 2</h3><a id="user-content-weathernext-2-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: WeatherNext 2" href="#weathernext-2-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>WeatherNext2_&lt;2025</strong> (Used Operationally): 0.25° resolution (~30km).
Fine-tuned on ECMWF HRES data and designed to be initialized directly from
operational HRES initial conditions rather than ERA5 reanalysis. Trained on
data through 2024. Corresponding weights files:
<code>WeatherNext2_&lt;2025_model{1,2,3,4}.npz</code>.</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">WeatherNext Cyclones - models which reproduce the results in paper</h3><a id="user-content-weathernext-cyclones---models-which-reproduce-the-results-in-paper" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: WeatherNext Cyclones - models which reproduce the results in paper" href="#weathernext-cyclones---models-which-reproduce-the-results-in-paper"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>WeatherNextCyclones_&lt;2025</strong> (Used Operationally): 0.25° resolution. The
model that ran live during the 2025 Atlantic hurricane season, publicly
referred to as FNV3 (NHC's postprocessed version was called GDMI). Trained
on data through 2024. The paper appendix contains a partial evaluation of
2025 in NHC basins for this model checkpoint, and how the tracker
improvement in September 2025 improved results. Corresponding weights files:
<code>WeatherNextCyclones_&lt;2025_model{1,2,3,4}.npz</code>.</li>
<li><strong>WeatherNextCyclones_&lt;2024</strong>: 0.25° resolution. Reproduces results from the
paper on 2024. Trained on data through 2023. Corresponding weights files:
<code>WeatherNextCyclones_&lt;2024_model{1,2,3,4}.npz</code>.</li>
<li><strong>WeatherNextCyclones_&lt;2023</strong>: 0.25° resolution. Reproduces results from the
paper on 2023. Trained on data through 2022. Corresponding weights files:
<code>WeatherNextCyclones_&lt;2023_model{1,2,3,4}.npz</code>.</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">WeatherNext Cyclones Mini</h3><a id="user-content-weathernext-cyclones-mini" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: WeatherNext Cyclones Mini" href="#weathernext-cyclones-mini"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>WeatherNextCyclones_Mini_&lt;2024</strong>: 1° resolution. A lightweight version
suitable for lower memory and compute constraints (e.g., local testing or
single TPUs or GPUs). Not expected to match the performance of the larger
versions. Forecasts the same things as WeatherNext2_&lt;2025, including
cyclones. Trained on data through 2023. Corresponding weights file:
<code>WeatherNextCyclones_Mini_&lt;2024.npz</code>.</li>
<li><strong>WeatherNextCyclones_Mini_&lt;2023</strong>: As above, but only trained on data through
2022. Corresponding weights file: <code>WeatherNextCyclones_Mini_&lt;2023.npz</code>.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Evaluation results for WeatherNextCyclones_Mini can be found in the appendix
of the
<a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10953-2" rel="nofollow">WeatherNext Cyclones Paper</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start Guide</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start-guide" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start Guide" href="#quick-start-guide"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The easiest way to get started with WeatherNext 2 is by running our interactive
<a href="/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/main/docs/weathernext2/wn2_demo.ipynb">Colab Notebook</a>, which can be opened from
<a href="https://colab.research.google.com/github/google-deepmind/weathernext/blob/master/docs/weathernext2/wn2_demo.ipynb" rel="nofollow">Colaboratory</a>.
This notebook defaults to WeatherNext Cyclones Mini, which we recommend running
using the <code>v5e-1</code> runtime, available for free as a Colab runtime. However, the
notebook can also be used to run the other models enumerated above (but these
will require a <code>v5p</code> accelerator).</p>
<p dir="auto">In general, we recommend running WeatherNext 2 on TPU where possible, since its
implementation has been optimised for it. However, if choosing to run on GPU,
the attention implementation must be switched, as shown in the demo notebook.
The non-Mini models require H100 for sufficient VRAM. The Mini models should
manage inference on a P100.</p>
<p dir="auto">Pre-trained weights and sample data are available on our <a href="https://console.cloud.google.com/storage/browser/dm_graphcast" rel="nofollow">Google Cloud
Bucket</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Inside the notebook, you will learn how to:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Automatically load the required model weights from our storage bucket.</li>
<li>Load initial state weather data (e.g., HRES initial conditions).</li>
<li>Initialize the WN2 (FGN) architecture.</li>
<li>Run auto-regressive rollout steps to generate a forecast prediction.</li>
<li>Visualize the outputs (e.g., temperature, wind speed, geopotential height).</li>
<li>Run the direct tracker on model outputs to obtain track data for cyclones.</li>
<li>Compute the training loss on model predictions and targets, and take a
gradient step.</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Setup</h2><a id="user-content-setup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Setup" href="#setup"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">E.g.:</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Model Weights</h3><a id="user-content-model-weights" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Model Weights" href="#model-weights"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To run WeatherNext 2 or WeatherNext Cyclones, you will need to download the
pre-trained model weights. You can access the weights on <a href="https://console.cloud.google.com/storage/browser/dm_graphcast" rel="nofollow">Google Cloud
Bucket</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Shared Utilities</h3><a id="user-content-shared-utilities" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Shared Utilities" href="#shared-utilities"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>utils/</code> directory contains shared libraries used by multiple WeatherNext
models, providing common infrastructure for autoregressive rollouts, input
normalization, graph building blocks, loss computation, and JAX-compatible
xarray utilities. See the per-model READMEs for model-specific code.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Training Data</h3><a id="user-content-training-data" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Training Data" href="#training-data"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Full model training requires downloading the
<a href="https://www.ecmwf.int/en/forecasts/datasets/reanalysis-datasets/era5" rel="nofollow">ERA5</a>
dataset from <a href="https://www.ecmwf.int/" rel="nofollow">ECMWF</a>, best accessed as Zarr via
<a href="https://weatherbench2.readthedocs.io/en/latest/data-guide.html#era5" rel="nofollow">WeatherBench2</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Operational fine-tuning data is available via <a href="https://weatherbench2.readthedocs.io/en/latest/data-guide.html#ifs-hres-t-0-analysis" rel="nofollow">WeatherBench2's HRES
data</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">These datasets may be governed by separate terms and conditions. Check that you
can comply with any applicable restrictions before use.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Acknowledgements</h2><a id="user-content-acknowledgements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Acknowledgements" href="#acknowledgements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The WeatherNext models communicate with the following separate libraries and
packages:.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Data and products of the European Centre for Medium-range Weather Forecasts
(ECMWF), as modified by Google.</li>
<li>Modified Copernicus Climate Change Service information 2023.</li>
<li>NOAA's International Best Track Archive for Climate Stewardship (IBTrACS)
data, first accessed on 1 Dec 2022.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Additionally, the colab notebooks include a few examples of ECMWF’s ERA5 and
HRES data that can be used as input to the models.</p>
<p dir="auto">Neither the European Commission nor ECMWF is responsible for any use that may be
made of the Copernicus information or data it contains. ECMWF HRES datasets
Copyright statement: Copyright "© 2023 European Centre for Medium-Range Weather
Forecasts (ECMWF)". Source: <a href="http://www.ecmwf.int/" rel="nofollow">www.ecmwf.int</a> License
Statement: ECMWF open data is published under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0
International (CC BY 4.0).
<a href="https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/" rel="nofollow">https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/</a>
Disclaimer: ECMWF does not accept any liability whatsoever for any error or
omission in the data, their availability, or for any loss or damage arising from
their use.</p>
<p dir="auto">Use of the third-party materials referred to above may be governed by separate
terms and conditions or license provisions. Your use of the third-party
materials is subject to any such terms and you should check that you can comply
with any applicable restrictions or terms and conditions before use.</p>

  </cookie-consent-link>
</li>

  <li class="mx-2">
    <cookie-consent-link>
      <button
        type="button"
        class="Link--secondary underline-on-hover border-0 p-0 color-bg-transparent text-left"
        data-action="click:cookie-consent-link#showConsentManagement"
        data-analytics-event="{&quot;location&quot;:&quot;footer&quot;,&quot;action&quot;:&quot;dont_share_info&quot;,&quot;context&quot;:&quot;subfooter&quot;,&quot;tag&quot;:&quot;link&quot;,&quot;label&quot;:&quot;dont_share_info_link_subfooter_footer&quot;}"
      >
        Do not share my personal information
      </button>
    </cookie-consent-link>
  </li>

      </ul>
    </nav>
  </div>
</footer>
